- **mentor**: Start here. Onboards the person, sets how Dev Mentor talks to them (experience level, tone, pace, build mode such as guided, copy, AI-written, spec or OpenSpec, and ADHD mode), suggests where to start in this codebase, routes to the right workflow, saves and resumes several lessons, exports lessons as notes to Notion, Obsidian or Markdown, and watches a PR's comments and CI. Use for "help", "where do I start?", "settings", "what are my settings?", "revert", "save", "resume", "lessons", "export lesson", or any mentoring request without a chosen workflow. (Dev Mentor) ([SirL0fty/dev-mentor](https://github.com/SirL0fty/dev-mentor/tree/HEAD/skills/mentor))
- **mentor**: Initialize and guide a structured learning project on any subject. Runs a Problem Framer Wizard, scaffolds a study project with a schedule, tracks progress through gates, mentors with Socratic guidance, and runs voice-animated roleplay of client/interviewer dialogue.
